dtp_ph_z_zone_agrarian_anglers = {
	num_zones = { type = zone_agrarian_anglers value > 0 }
}

dtp_pok_z_zone_agrarian_anglers = {
	exists = owner owner = { has_valid_civic = civic_agrarian_idyll is_anglers_empire = yes } is_wet = yes
	hidden_trigger = { exists = owner owner = { has_valid_civic = civic_agrarian_idyll is_anglers_empire = yes } is_wet = yes }
	OR = {
		has_district = district_farming
		has_district = district_rw_farming
		has_district = district_orchard_forests
	}
}

dtp_ph_z_zone_agrarian_urban = {
	num_zones = { type = zone_agrarian_urban value > 0 }
}

dtp_pok_z_zone_agrarian_urban = {
	exists = owner owner = { has_valid_civic = civic_agrarian_idyll } if = { limit = { owner = { is_anglers_empire = yes } } is_wet = no }
	hidden_trigger = { exists = owner owner = { has_valid_civic = civic_agrarian_idyll } if = { limit = { owner = { is_anglers_empire = yes } } is_wet = no } }
	OR = {
		has_district = district_farming
		has_district = district_rw_farming
		has_district = district_orchard_forests
	}
}

dtp_ph_z_zone_anglers = {
	num_zones = { type = zone_anglers value > 0 }
}

dtp_pok_z_zone_anglers = {
	hidden_trigger = { exists = owner owner = { is_anglers_empire = yes } } OR = { is_wet = yes owner = { is_nomadic = yes } }
	hidden_trigger = { exists = owner owner = { is_anglers_empire = yes } } OR = { is_wet = yes owner = { is_nomadic = yes } }
	OR = {
		has_district = district_farming
		has_district = district_rw_farming
		has_district = district_orchard_forests
	}
}

dtp_ph_z_zone_ark_ammunition_lines = {
